# Day 01 — 2026-08-25

Zone 35 · 4,266 arc traversals · 7.94 h driving + 0.75 h break = **8.69 h**

Capture 3.99 h · deadhead 2.28 h · turns 1.67 h


Start and finish at the depot. **Capture** rows are required footage: keep to 40 km/h or below unless the row says *reduced*. **Deadhead** rows are repositioning — drive them normally, the camera is not counted.


> **262 streets on this route are marked [HOLD 40 — still owed].** You would otherwise have driven past them; filming them today is what saves a return trip later in the campaign. Hold to the capture speed on those rows. If you cannot — traffic, a closure, anything — say so, because the street then goes back on the list and the plan needs to know.
